- **agent-memory**: SessionEnd, SessionStart, UserPromptSubmit on *. Three hooks implementing the L0-L3 memory tiers. SessionStart injects L3 (global persona) + L2 (current project context). UserPromptSubmit recalls relevant L1 atoms. SessionEnd asynchronously captures L0 into L1, detects contradictions, and stages promotions for human adoption.
